<div class="news-content">
<div class="news-info news-active" content-id="1" style="display: block;">
<h4>New Manufacturer: BEC Technologies</h4>
<p>Baltic Networks is delighted  to announce that we have entered a distribution partnership with BEC Technologies. BEC Technologies is a leading developer and manufacturer of next generation wired &amp; wireless IP networking solutions for mobile operators, residential, enterprise, and Industrial markets. BEC Technologies product portfolio of solutions incorporate xDSL, FTTH, 4G/LTE, Fixed Data Routers, CBRS, Public Safety, and Cloud-based remote device management.</p>
<a href="https://www.balticnetworks.com/manufacturers/bec-technologies.html"><img src="https://www.balticnetworks.com/images/btn_viewproducts.jpg"/> </a>
</div>
<div class="news-info" content-id="2" style="display: none;">
<h4>New Manufacturer: PoE Texas</h4>
<p>Baltic Networks is proud to announce a distribution partnership with PoE Texas. PoE Texas delivers Power over Ethernet solutions that expand the potential of your IT infrastructure without replacing it. With more than 150 products designed to integrate with existing network technology, PoE Texas has made the promise of PoE accessible to businesses all around the world. </p>
<a href="https://www.balticnetworks.com/manufacturers/poe-texas.html"><img src="https://www.balticnetworks.com/images/btn_viewproducts.jpg"/> </a>
</div>
<div class="news-info" content-id="3" style="display: none;">
<h4>New Manufacturer: BLiNQ Networks</h4>
<p>Baltic Networks is proud to announce that we have entered into a distribution agreement with BLiNQ Networks. BLiNQ Networks is the industry leading CBRS provider and their fixed wireless access solutions support LTE TDD bands 40/41/42/43/46/48 to help service providers build, expand, and optimize their networks at an affordable cost.</p>
<a href="https://www.balticnetworks.com/manufacturers/blinq-networks.html"><img src="https://www.balticnetworks.com/images/btn_viewproducts.jpg"/> </a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script charset="utf-8" type="text/javascript">
 var rand = Math.floor(Math.random()*3) + 1;
 jQuery(function(){
  jQuery('[content-id='+rand+']').addClass('news-active');
  jQuery('.news-tab').on('click', function(){
   jQuery('.news-tab').removeClass('news-active');
   jQuery('.news-img img').hide();
   jQuery('.news-info').hide();
   var contentId = jQuery(this).attr('content-id');
   jQuery('.news-img img[content-id='+contentId+']').fadeIn('fast');
   jQuery('.news-info[content-id='+contentId+']').fadeIn('fast');
   jQuery(this).addClass('news-active');
   
   return false;
  });
 
  jQuery('.news-active').trigger('click');
 });
 
   // start twitter feed
 new Ajax.Request('https://www.balticnetworks.com/twitter.php', {
    onSuccess: function(response) {
    var obj = response.responseText.evalJSON();
    for(i=0; i<obj.length; i++){
     var text = formatAnchor(obj[i].text);
     var tweet = '<p class="tweet">'+text+'</p>';
     $('twitter').insert(tweet);
    }
    $$('p.tweet').each(function(e){
     e.hide();
    });
    
    $$('p.tweet:first').each(function(e){
     e.appear().addClassName('active');
    });
    setInterval(function(){tweets()},7000);
    
  }
 });
 function tweets(){
  $$('p.active').each(function(e){
   e.removeClassName('active');
   var next = e.next();
   if(!next){
    next = $$('p.tweet:first')[0];
   }
  e.fade({
   afterFinish: function(){
     next.appear().addClassName('active');
    }
   });
    
   });
  }
  function formatAnchor(text) {
   var exp = /(\b(https?|ftp|file):\/\/[-A-Z0-9+&@#\/%?=~_|!:,.;]*[-A-Z0-9+&@#\/%=~_|])/ig;
   return text.replace(exp,"<a target='_blank' href='$1'>$1</a>"); 
  }
 </script>

    clearInterval(interval);
    interval = setInterval(function () {

        if (delay1571764088 < 30) {

            var buttons = $$('.ajaxpro-continue-button');

                clearInterval(interval);
                return;

<script type="text/javascript">
document.observe('dom:loaded', function() {
    $('scroll-up').hide();
    Event.observe(window, 'scroll', function() {
        if (document.viewport.getScrollOffsets()[1] > 180) {
            $('scroll-up').show();
        } else {
            $('scroll-up').hide();
        }
    });

    $('scroll-up').down('a').observe('click', function(e) {
        e.stop();
        Effect.ScrollTo(document.body, { duration:'0.2' });
        return false;
    });
});
</script>
